HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_10e3e35edc5b7c8c605c","text":"1. **Suspended negation between death and life.** Lexical evidence: موت B001 loss of life/vitality; حيي B003 animate living being. Structural cue: repeated لا around two opposed verbs inside \"فيها.\" Assigned role: موت and حياة are both denied, trapping the subject in a non-resolution. Abductive move: the packet supplies the life/death opposition; I infer suspension from double negation. Reading change: punishment is not simple death but blocked completion of either state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1d2d0d0046a3977080bc","text":"3. **Submission refused.** Lexical evidence: م و ت B013 (submission to truth), ذ ك ر B009 in 87:9, خ ش ي B001/B002 in 87:10. Structural cue: the one who avoids reminder reaches this suspended state. Assigned role: \"does not die\" may also imply he never performs the truth-yielding death/submission that would have answered reminder. Abductive move: existential suspension mirrors moral non-submission. Reading change: the negated death includes a missed surrender, not only biological continuation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_209428d58a181ca28d77","text":"87:14 immediately reversed the suspension: \"قد أفلح من تزكى\" shows the positive path where growth/purity succeeds. 87:16-17 added that the avoided life is paradoxical: choosing الحياة الدنيا can end in a state that is neither death nor life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2b4660608205a5fecadd","text":"87:14’s purification-growth and 87:17’s good persistence corrected an undifferentiated “endless life” reading. They show exactly what the fixed state lacks: productive renewal and beneficial continuance. This also made the dormant submission branch `م و ت/B013` newly visible as a continuation of the earlier refusal to respond.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B013","root_001454/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_397ace40bb5a0bdf99ff","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:13:3\",\"root_001454\",[\"B006\"]],[\"87:13:6\",\"root_000383\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Öğüdü reddeden kişi, kalbin ölüleşmesiyle kurtarıcı hayatı yitirmiş fakat ölümle serbest kalamamış halde görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3f68dd48f351306f6d38","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:13:3\",\"root_001454\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ölümün teslim olma çağrışımı, kişinin ateşte bile gerçeğe boyun eğmeyen önceki kaçınmasını sürdürdüğünü düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B001","root_001454/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_43abce4da8da5da39e1a","text":"3. **Exploratory: refusal to yield becomes fixed.** `م و ت/B013` is submission to truth, a dormant branch activated by reminder, recollection, and avoidance in 87:9–11. Negating `يَمُوتُ` can therefore add the image of one who still does not yield, while negating `يَحْيَىٰ` denies the benefit that yielding might open. The primary death sense remains supplied by `م و ت/B001`; this secondary layer depends on root resonance rather than the immediate form. It makes the condition a frozen continuation of the earlier avoidance, not merely an imposed environment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4a8b482e0c37f3e53321","text":"2. **Dead land versus rain-life.** Lexical evidence: م و ت B003 (dead land/lifeless property), ح ي ي B002 (earth alive by rain and growth), ر ع ي B001 in 87:4 and غ ث و B002 in 87:5. Structural cue: pasture lives then dries; here the person neither dies nor lives. Assigned role: ecological life/death cycles are withheld in the fire. Abductive move: natural renewal is a mercy not available inside the punitive state. Reading change: the fire becomes anti-ecology: no fertile death, no living renewal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_56946cf93123593fb2ed","text":"2. **Deadness of heart and life as saving benefit.** Lexical evidence: موت B006 deadness of heart; حيي B013 life as benefit/saving good; نفع B001 benefit in 87:9. Structural cue: the avoidant refused beneficial reminder before the fire. Assigned role: he loses the benefit-life offered by reminder yet does not receive release by death. Abductive move: the packet supplies benefit-life and heart-deadness; I infer that rejection produces inner lifelessness before final suspension. Reading change: the ayah becomes moral-existential, not only biological.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5d40396be2f1a3addd97","text":"4. **False submission and unreleased truth.** Lexical evidence: موت B013 submission to truth and B011 feigned humility; ذكر B009 reminder; خشي B001 awe. Structural cue: the one who avoided reminder contrasts with the one who fears. Assigned role: موت's submission branch reveals the missing act: he neither truly submits nor truly exits. Abductive move: the packet supplies submission and feigned deadness; I infer that avoidance leaves him unable to perform the death-to-truth that would have saved him. Reading change: the ayah encodes failed surrender, not only torment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B013","root_001454/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8082500f3a29b381f075","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:13:3\",\"root_001454\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:13:6\",\"root_000383\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ölümün kapanışı da hayatın kurtarıcı yararı da engellendiği için durum iki hal arasında çözümsüz bir askıda kalıştır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B003","root_001454/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_826e43610eec8da4e8b9","text":"1. **Suspended negation between death and life.** Lexical evidence: م و ت B001/B012 (loss of vitality; stillness), ح ي ي B003/B013 (living creature; life as saving good). Structural cue: paired negations hold the subject inside the fire after 87:12. Assigned role: death is denied as release; life is denied as benefit. Abductive move: punishment can suspend ordinary endings and goods. Reading change: the ayah describes not paradox for its own sake but a blocked transition-state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8b88d75e8cb08be6ae3f","text":"87:14 immediately reversed the suspended state: success/purification offers genuine life-direction after the non-life of fire. The earlier pasture cycle also made this ayah feel like an interruption of normal life/death renewal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_98c20490f1b464b70539","text":"3. **No regeneration after the pasture cycle.** Lexical evidence: حيي B002 earth brought alive by rain/growth; غثو B002 dried pasture/refuse in 87:5; موت B003 dead land/lifeless property. Structural cue: earlier vegetation moved from pasture to refuse; here life/death are both denied. Assigned role: the ecological cycle is broken in the fire. Abductive move: the packet supplies land revived by rain and dead land; I infer that this state lacks the rain-growth renewal available in creation. Reading change: \"nor live\" means no new greening, no productive return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e1f17f303bc420f77480","text":"1. **Both terminal closure and beneficial life are denied.** `م و ت/B001` is loss of vitality and life, while `ح ي ي/B013` is life as benefit, saving good, or rescue from destruction. The balanced negations follow exposure to fire and precede `أَفْلَحَ`, `خَيْرٌ`, and `أَبْقَىٰ`. Death would terminate the process; life would convert it into benefit. I infer those functional roles from the contrasted sequel. The ayah reads as exclusion from both closure and rescue, not as a simple paradox of biological status.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B002","root_001454/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f6c1eb23a487ae72e4b8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:13:3\",\"root_001454\",[\"B012\"]],[\"87:13:6\",\"root_000383\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Toprağın yeniden canlanması çağrışımı da reddedildiğinden, bu süreklilik dinlenmeye varamayan ve yeni hayat üretemeyen kısır bir süreçtir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001454/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f7df4ff32bcd22a4d26e","text":"2. **The state is nonproductive suspension.** `م و ت/B012` gives stillness or lifeless inactivity, while `ح ي ي/B002` gives earth brought alive through rain and fresh growth. Later `ز ك و/B001` supplies increase and `ب ق ي/B001` enduring continuation, making productive renewal visible by contrast. The two negations block both settled stillness and regenerative growth. I infer that what remains is ongoing but sterile agitation. The reading changes from an abstract midpoint into a process that neither comes to rest nor yields new life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
